### getAwaitingJobsWithInfo()
#### Timings
  - invocations: 197
  - total duration: PT16.869808593S
  - avg duration: PT0.085633546S
  - min duration: PT0.002578678S
  - max duration: PT0.227832125S

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select ns.*, j.jobAsJson from(     select j.id, j.awaitingOn AS awaitedJobId, p.state AS awaitedJobState         FROM jobrunr_jobs j INNER JOIN jobrunr_jobs p ON p.id = j.awaitingOn         WHERE j.state = 'AWAITING' AND j.rateLimiter IS NULL AND (j.awaitingOnState = p.state OR p.state  IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED','DELETED','NOT_APPLICABLE'))  ORDER BY j.rateLimiter ASC, j.priority ASC, j.updatedAt ASC LIMIT :limit) ns, jobrunr_jobs j WHERE j.id = ns.id`
 - Query with values: `select ns.*, j.jobAsJson from(     select j.id, j.awaitingOn AS awaitedJobId, p.state AS awaitedJobState         FROM jobrunr_jobs j INNER JOIN jobrunr_jobs p ON p.id = j.awaitingOn         WHERE j.state = 'AWAITING' AND j.rateLimiter IS NULL AND (j.awaitingOnState = p.state OR p.state  IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED','DELETED','NOT_APPLICABLE'))  ORDER BY j.rateLimiter ASC, j.priority ASC, j.updatedAt ASC LIMIT 1024) ns, jobrunr_jobs j WHERE j.id = ns.id`
   - timeToFirstResultSet query part timings: invocations: 197 / total duration: PT7.894716127S / avg duration: PT0.040074701S / min duration: PT0.000823802S / max duration: PT0.150847737S

### getBatchJobsWithChildStats()
#### Timings
  - invocations: 21
  - total duration: PT12.514917314S
  - avg duration: PT0.595948443S
  - min duration: PT0.175433211S
  - max duration: PT1.156731444S

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= '2025-10-16 10:35:15.863' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
   - timeToFirstResultSet query part timings: invocations: 21 / total duration: PT11.485003149S / avg duration: PT0.546904911S / min duration: PT0.174752181S / max duration: PT0.992308834S
   - prepareTime query part timings: invocations: 21 / total duration: PT0.000074924S / avg duration: PT0.000003567S / min duration: PT0.000001275S / max duration: PT0.000013505S
   - totalTime query part timings: invocations: 21 / total duration: PT11.500579606S / avg duration: PT0.547646647S / min duration: PT0.17478783S / max duration: PT0.992829325S
   - executeTime query part timings: invocations: 21 / total duration: PT11.484928225S / avg duration: PT0.546901344S / min duration: PT0.174738676S / max duration: PT0.992306481S
